## 2026-10-22 02:4x strategy review: W45N7 rhauler respawn clusters (`docs/REMOTE_W45N7.md` "02:4x")
- **Found:** the repeated "home storage below the 15k floor after a respawn cluster" (patrol 14:5x, 22:2x, 01:5x) is structural, not a one-off. Live t83789510 the 5 W45N7 rhaulers had TTL 955/895/835/752/684, i.e. all within 271 ticks (ideal 1500/5 = 300 apart). `rhGap: 60` only keeps births 60t apart, so once an Invader pause or wipe respawns them together they stay bunched for good and every lifetime re-spawns 5x800e (~4k e) inside ~300t, pinning storage under the floor and throttling home upgrade for that window. Overmind/TooAngel handle this the same way: spread replacements by spawn time/lifetime instead of a fixed short gap.
- **Shipped 769971b (deployed t83789528):** `C.rhGapSteady` (W45N7 240): while at most one rhauler is missing, births must be >=240t apart, so the cluster spreads to ~300t spacing within one lifetime; with >=2 missing (post-wipe) the old 60t gap still applies. Steady state is unaffected (natural spacing 300 > 240). Cost: during the next ~1500t some replacements arrive up to ~170t late, leaving 4 of 5 haulers briefly (they were 46-82% waiting_pickup at 5, container buffers). Tests/check pass, 0 errors after deploy.
- **Patrol check:** W45N7 rhauler TTL spread should widen each cycle (target min gap >=200 by ~10-22 05:00); remote container must not overflow; W45N7 delivered must not drop >0.5 e/t. Kill switch: remove `rhGapSteady` from `managers/remote.js` CFG.
- **Plan check (t83789534):** home RCL6 1.375M/3.645M at ~20-21 e/t, ~4.4 s/tick -> RCL7 ~10-27 14:00-22:00 UTC+8 (+O ~5 h), still before novice end 10-28 03:33 but the margin is shrinking (~5-13 h); smoother storage from this fix helps a little. W47N8 RCL5 729.8k/1.215M at ~25 e/t -> RCL6 ~10-23 02:00. CPU bucket 10000, hostiles 0, BustJetter armed group still parked in W48N9. No decision for Misaki.

## 2026-10-20 02:4x strategy review: home hauler at RCL6, console query size (`docs/MODEL.md` "02:4x")

The 23:5x open question is closed.
- **Console query size (tools only, no deploy):** the server accepts 1001-char console expressions and refuses 1024 with `{'error': 'expression size is too large'}`. `tools/q.py` ignored that reply and waited ~65 s, so an oversized query looked like a stalled listener (the 02:18 restart; the 02:41 patrol suspected the same). q.py now checks the wrapped size locally (max 1000, about 890 chars of user expression) and raises any console error at once. **Patrols:** a `q.py: expression too large` or `console refused` message means split the query, not restart the listener. The listener watchdog idea below stays open only for a real no-reply on a small query.
- Plan check: W47N8 RCL5 ~10-20 08:00-09:00 (tower 24,8 site first), home RCL7 ~10-26 02:00, both before novice end 10-28 03:33. No change to build orders, defence or CPU budget.

Energy asks ~38 cr at 0.65 e/unit = **~107 cr per NET delivered energy**. The O -> credits -> energy round trip is **net negative (~-320 e per 1000 O)**. **Never buy energy from here.**
